- **certora-autoprover**: End-to-end formal verification of a Solidity contract with the Certora Prover. Given a project (Foundry/Hardhat) and a main contract, this skill analyzes the system's components, runs certora-autosetup to build a working prover configuration and summaries, formulates security properties (invariants, safety properties, attack vectors) per component, formalizes them in CVL, and iterates certoraRun until every rule is verified, excused as a suspected bug, or skipped with justification — ending with a verification report. ([Certora/autoprover-skill](https://github.com/Certora/autoprover-skill/tree/HEAD/plugins/certora-autoprover) · ★9 · 1 skill)
